# Graph Report - uca-l0869  (2026-10-22)

## Corpus Check
- 2045 files · ~4,182,034 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 42698 nodes · 88631 edges · 2067 communities (1669 shown, 338 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1742 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `7aea9d57`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
